// js/edr.js
(function() {
    const content = document.getElementById('edrContent');
    const terminalWrapper = document.querySelector('.terminal-wrapper'); // Наш контейнер-рамка
    
    const osPassed = localStorage.getItem('s9_edr_os_passed') === 'true';
    const gatewayPassed = localStorage.getItem('s9_edr_gateway_passed') === 'true';
    const osTrigger = localStorage.getItem('s9_edr_os_trigger') === 'true';
    const gatewayTrigger = localStorage.getItem('s9_edr_gateway_trigger') === 'true';

    if (window.edrScanInterval) clearInterval(window.edrScanInterval);
    if (window.edrLogInterval) clearInterval(window.edrLogInterval);

    if (osTrigger) {
        startSandbox();
    } 
    else if (gatewayTrigger) {
        startMastermind();
    } 
    else {
        if (osPassed && gatewayPassed) {
            initShieldVisual(false);
        } else if (osPassed || gatewayPassed) {
            initShieldVisual(true, osPassed ? 'ОС' : 'ШЛЮЗ');
        } else {
            window.location.replace('404.html');
        }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // ЛОГИКА ПЕСОЧНИЦЫ (ЧЕРЕЗ КОНСОЛЬ)
    // ==========================================
    function startSandbox() {
        // ПОЛНОСТЬЮ СКРЫВАЕМ РАМКУ И КОНТЕНТ, ЧТОБЫ НЕ МЕШАЛИ КОНСОЛИ
        if (terminalWrapper) terminalWrapper.style.display = 'none';
        if (content) content.style.display = 'none';

        let currentLevel = 1;
        let errorCount = 0;
        let correctAnswer = 0;

        // Блокируем консоль и открываем её
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (window.printToConsole) {
                window.printToConsole(`<span class="warn">[EDR] ЗАПУЩЕНА ПЕСОЧНИЦА. ВСЕ ОТВЕТЫ ВВОДИТЕ В КОНСОЛИ.</span>`);
                window.dispatchEvent(new Event('lockConsole'));
                generateLevel(1);
            }
        }, 500);

        const rand = (min, max) => Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min;

        function generateLevel(level) {
            errorCount = 0;
            let codeHtml = '';
            
            if (level === 1) {
                const startX = rand(3, 10); // Расширено
                const addVal = rand(2, 8);  // Расширено
                const multVal = rand(2, 4); // Расширено
                const loops = rand(2, 4);   // Случайное число циклов
                
                let ans = startX;
                for(let i=0; i<loops; i++) { ans += addVal; ans *= multVal; }
                correctAnswer = ans;

                codeHtml = `
                    <div class="console-code-block">
                        <span class="kw">LET</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="num">${startX}</span><br>
                        <span class="kw">LOOP</span> <span class="num">${loops}</span> TIMES:<br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">+</span> <span class="num">${addVal}</span><br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">*</span> <span class="num">${multVal}</span><br>
                        <span class="kw">PRINT</span> <span class="var">X</span>
                    </div>
                `;
            } 
            else if (level === 2) {
                const startX = rand(5, 15);    // Расширено
                const addVal = rand(3, 9);     // Расширено
                const threshold = rand(20, 35); // Расширено
                const loops = rand(2, 4);      // Случайное число циклов
                
                let ans = startX;
                for(let i=0; i<loops; i++) { ans += addVal; if (ans > threshold) ans *= 2; }
                correctAnswer = ans;

                codeHtml = `
                    <div class="console-code-block">
                        <span class="kw">LET</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="num">${startX}</span><br>
                        <span class="kw">LOOP</span> <span class="num">${loops}</span> TIMES:<br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">+</span> <span class="num">${addVal}</span><br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kw">IF</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">></span> <span class="num">${threshold}</span>:<br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">*</span> <span class="num">2</span><br>
                        <span class="kw">PRINT</span> <span class="var">X</span>
                    </div>
                `;
            } 
            else if (level === 3) {
                const startX = rand(2, 6);
                const startY = rand(10, 20);
                const multVal = rand(2, 4);
                const loops = rand(2, 4);
                
                let x = startX, y = startY;
                for(let i=0; i<loops; i++) {
                    x *= multVal;
                    if (x > 20) y += x;
                    else if (x > 10) y *= 2;
                    else y -= 3;
                }
                correctAnswer = y;

                codeHtml = `
                    <div class="console-code-block">
                        <span class="kw">LET</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="num">${startX}</span><br>
                        <span class="kw">LET</span> <span class="var">Y</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="num">${startY}</span><br>
                        <span class="kw">LOOP</span> <span class="num">${loops}</span> TIMES:<br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">*</span> <span class="num">${multVal}</span><br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kw">IF</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">></span> <span class="num">20</span>:<br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="var">Y</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="var">Y</span> <span class="op">+</span> <span class="var">X</span><br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kw">ELSE IF</span> <span class="var">X</span> <span class="op">></span> <span class="num">10</span>:<br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="var">Y</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="var">Y</span> <span class="op">*</span> <span class="num">2</span><br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kw">ELSE</span>:<br>
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="var">Y</span> <span class="op">=</span> <span class="var">Y</span> <span class="op">-</span> <span class="num">3</span><br>
                        <span class="kw">PRINT</span> <span class="var">Y</span>
                    </div>
                `;
            }

            // Выводим в консоль
            if (window.printToConsole) {
                window.printToConsole(`<span class="topic-header">[EDR] ЭТАП ${level} / 3</span>`);
                window.printToConsole(codeHtml);
                window.printToConsole(`ВВЕДИТЕ РЕЗУЛЬТАТ: <span class="cmd-text">edr --answer <ЧИСЛО></span>`);
            }
        }

        // Экспортируем функцию для консоли
        window.checkEdrAnswer = function(val) {
            const num = parseInt(val, 10);
            
            if (num === correctAnswer) {
                if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">> ЛОГИКА ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНА.</span>`);
                
                if (currentLevel < 3) {
                    currentLevel++;
                    setTimeout(() => generateLevel(currentLevel), 1000);
                } else {
                    localStorage.setItem('s9_edr_os_passed', 'true');
                    localStorage.removeItem('s9_edr_os_trigger');
                    localStorage.setItem('s9_os_fixed', 'true');
                    
                    // Лечим текущую структуру кубитов, сохраняя позиции серых
                    let currentQubits = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('s9_os_qubits_data') || 'null');
                    if (currentQubits) {
                        for (let l = 1; l <= 5; l++) {
                            for (let r = 1; r <= 5; r++) {
                                for (let c = 1; c <= 5; c++) {
                                    if (currentQubits[l]?.[r]?.[c] && currentQubits[l][r][c].state !== 'locked') {
                                        currentQubits[l][r][c].state = 'active';
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        localStorage.setItem('s9_os_qubits_data', JSON.stringify(currentQubits));
                    }

                    if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">> ВСЕ ТЕСТЫ ПРОЙДЕНЫ. ВОЗВРАТ В ОС...</span>`);
                    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('unlockConsole'));
                    setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = 'os.html'; }, 2000);
                }
            } else {
                errorCount++;
                if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">> ОШИБКА ${errorCount}/3.</span>`);

                if (errorCount >= 3) {
                    if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="warn">> СБРОС ПАМЯТИ. ГЕНЕРАЦИЯ НОВОГО ЗАДАНИЯ...</span>`);
                    setTimeout(() => generateLevel(currentLevel), 1500);
                }
            }
        };
    }

    // ==========================================
    // ЛОГИКА ВЗЛОМА ШИФРА (Возвращаем рамку)
    // ==========================================
    function startMastermind() {
        if (terminalWrapper) terminalWrapper.style.display = 'flex';
        if (content) content.style.display = 'flex';

        const targetCode = Array.from({length: 5}, () => Math.floor(Math.random() * 10)).join('');
        content.innerHTML = `
            <div style="margin-bottom: 15px; color: var(--neon); text-align:center; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.5;">
                > ДОСТУП К ШЛЮЗУ ЗАБЛОКИРОВАН.<br>
                > ПОДБЕРИТЕ 5-ЗНАЧНЫЙ КОД.<br>
            </div>
            <div class="mm-logs" id="mmLogs" style="width: 100%; max-width: 320px; height: 120px; overflow-y: auto; border: 1px solid rgba(0,242,255,0.2); padding: 10px; font-size: 11px; margin-bottom: 15px; text-align: left; font-family: monospace; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5);">> СИСТЕМА ГОТОВА К ВВОДУ...</div>
            <div class="mm-inputs" id="mmInputs" style="display: flex; gap: 8px; justify-content: center; margin-bottom: 15px;">
                <input type="text" maxlength="1" oninput="moveFocus(this)" style="width: 35px; height: 45px; background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--neon); color: var(--neon); font-size: 20px; text-align: center; font-family: monospace; outline: none;"><input type="text" maxlength="1" oninput="moveFocus(this)" style="width: 35px; height: 45px; background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--neon); color: var(--neon); font-size: 20px; text-align: center; font-family: monospace; outline: none;"><input type="text" maxlength="1" oninput="moveFocus(this)" style="width: 35px; height: 45px; background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--neon); color: var(--neon); font-size: 20px; text-align: center; font-family: monospace; outline: none;"><input type="text" maxlength="1" oninput="moveFocus(this)" style="width: 35px; height: 45px; background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--neon); color: var(--neon); font-size: 20px; text-align: center; font-family: monospace; outline: none;"><input type="text" maxlength="1" oninput="moveFocus(this)" style="width: 35px; height: 45px; background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--neon); color: var(--neon); font-size: 20px; text-align: center; font-family: monospace; outline: none;">
            </div>
            <button id="mmBtn" class="mobile-btn" style="display: block; background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--neon); color: var(--neon); padding: 10px 20px; font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace; font-size: 12px; cursor: pointer;">ПРОВЕРИТЬ</button>
        `;
        window.moveFocus = (el) => { if (el.value.length === 1 && el.nextElementSibling) el.nextElementSibling.focus(); };

        const checkCode = () => {
            const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('#mmInputs input');
            let guess = ''; inputs.forEach(i => guess += i.value);
            if (guess.length !== 5) return;

            let exact = 0, wrongPos = 0;
            let targetArr = targetCode.split(''), guessArr = guess.split('');
            for(let i=0; i<5; i++) { if (guessArr[i] === targetArr[i]) { exact++; targetArr[i] = null; guessArr[i] = null; } }
            for(let i=0; i<5; i++) { if (guessArr[i] !== null) { const idx = targetArr.indexOf(guessArr[i]); if (idx !== -1) { wrongPos++; targetArr[idx] = null; } } }

            const log = document.getElementById('mmLogs');
            log.innerHTML += `<div>> ВВОД: ${guess} | <span style="color:#00ff44">ТОЧНО: ${exact}</span> | <span style="color:#ffaa00">НЕ ТАМ: ${wrongPos}</span></div>`;
            log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;

            if (exact === 5) {
                localStorage.setItem('s9_edr_gateway_passed', 'true');
                localStorage.removeItem('s9_edr_gateway_trigger');
                content.innerHTML = `<h2 style="color: #00ff44;">ШЛЮЗ РАЗБЛОКИРОВАН</h2><p>Возврат на Карту...</p>`;
                setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = 'map.html'; }, 2000);
            } else {
                inputs.forEach(i => i.value = '');
                inputs[0].focus();
            }
        };

        document.getElementById('mmBtn').onclick = checkCode;
        document.querySelectorAll('#mmInputs input').forEach((inp, idx, arr) => {
            inp.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if (idx < 4 && inp.value.length === 1) arr[idx+1].focus(); else checkCode();
                }
            });
        });

        setTimeout(() => document.querySelector('#mmInputs input').focus(), 100);
    }

    // ==========================================
    // РАБОЧИЙ РЕЖИМ (ЩИТ) - Возвращаем рамку
    // ==========================================
    function initShieldVisual(isPartial = false, moduleName = '') {
        if (terminalWrapper) terminalWrapper.style.display = 'flex';
        if (content) content.style.display = 'flex';

        content.innerHTML = `
            <div class="main-layout">
                <div class="shield-wrapper">
                    <div class="shield-shape" id="shieldGrid"></div>
                </div>
                <div class="side-panel">
                    <div class="counters">
                        <div class="counter-box">
                            <div class="counter-label">ПРОВЕРЕНО</div>
                            <div class="counter-value" id="cnt-scanned">0</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="counter-box">
                            <div class="counter-label">УГРОЗЫ</div>
                            <div class="counter-value" id="cnt-threats">0</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="counter-box">
                            <div class="counter-label">УСТРАНЕНО</div>
                            <div class="counter-value" id="cnt-cleared">0</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="log-container">
                        <div class="log-box" id="logBox"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;

        const grid = document.getElementById('shieldGrid');
        const logBox = document.getElementById('logBox');
        
        let totalScanned = 0, activeThreats = 0, totalCleared = 0;
        const elScanned = document.getElementById('cnt-scanned');
        const elThreats = document.getElementById('cnt-threats');
        const elCleared = document.getElementById('cnt-cleared');

        function formatNumber(num) {
            if (num >= 1000000) return (num / 1000000).toFixed(1) + 'M';
            if (num >= 1000) return (num / 1000).toFixed(1) + 'K';
            return num.toString();
        }

        const cells = [];
        const cols = 12, rows = 12;

        for (let i = 0; i < cols * rows; i++) {
            const cell = document.createElement('div');
            cell.className = 'shield-cell';
            grid.appendChild(cell);
            cells.push(cell);
        }

        function addLog(text, type = '') {
            const line = document.createElement('div');
            line.className = `log-line ${type}`;
            line.innerText = text;
            logBox.appendChild(line);
            if (logBox.children.length > 35) logBox.removeChild(logBox.firstChild);
        }

        for(let i=0; i<25; i++) addLog("> SYS_MONITOR: BACKGROUND OK");

        window.edrScanInterval = setInterval(() => {
            const idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * cells.length);
            const cell = cells[idx];

            if (!cell.classList.contains('threat') && !cell.classList.contains('cleared')) {
                cell.classList.add('scanning');
                setTimeout(() => cell.classList.remove('scanning'), 100);
                totalScanned++;
                elScanned.innerText = formatNumber(totalScanned);
            }

            if (Math.random() > 0.96) {
                const threatIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * cells.length);
                const threatCell = cells[threatIdx];

                if (!threatCell.classList.contains('threat') && !threatCell.classList.contains('cleared') && !threatCell.classList.contains('scanning')) {
                    threatCell.classList.add('threat');
                    activeThreats++;
                    elThreats.innerText = formatNumber(activeThreats);

                    const r = Math.floor(threatIdx / cols) + 1;
                    const c = (threatIdx % cols) + 1;

                    addLog(`> PID ${Math.floor(Math.random()*9000)+1000}: WARNING! THREAT AT [${r},${c}]`, 'warn');

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        threatCell.classList.remove('threat');
                        threatCell.classList.add('cleared');
                        activeThreats--;
                        totalCleared++;
                        elThreats.innerText = formatNumber(activeThreats);
                        elCleared.innerText = formatNumber(totalCleared);
                        addLog(`> PID: THREAT NEUTRALIZED.`, 'ok');
                        setTimeout(() => threatCell.classList.remove('cleared'), 1000);
                    }, 1500);
                }
            }
        }, 50);

        window.edrLogInterval = setInterval(() => {
            if (Math.random() > 0.6) {
                const messages = [
                    "> SCANNING KERNEL SPACE... 100%",
                    "> CHECKING INTEGRITY: SYS_CORE.DLL",
                    "> MEMORY BLOCK 0x4F2A: CLEAN",
                    "> NETWORK GATEWAY: PING OK",
                    "> CHECKING HASH SIGNATURES...",
                    "> ALL SYSTEMS SECURE"
                ];
                addLog(messages[Math.floor(Math.random() * messages.length)]);
            }
        }, 1000);
    }
})();

// js/glyph.js
// --- ГЕОМЕТРИЯ И ГЛИФЫ ---
function getPath(word) {
	word = word.toUpperCase(); // Добавь это ПЕРВЫМ делом
    let hash = 5381;
    for (let i = 0; i < word.length; i++) hash = ((hash << 5) + hash) + word.charCodeAt(i);
    let seed = Math.abs(hash), path = [], used = new Set();
    const len = (seed % 3) + 5;
    while(path.length < len && used.size < 9) {
        seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff;
        let n = seed % 9; if(!used.has(n)) { path.push(n); used.add(n); }
    }
    return path;
}

function createSVG(path) {
    if (!path || !path.length) return '';
    const start = POINTS[path[0]]; 
    let d = `M ${start.x} ${start.y}`;
    for(let i=0; i<path.length-1; i++) {
        const c = POINTS[path[i]], n = POINTS[path[i+1]];
        d += ` C ${c.x+(n.x-c.x)*0.5} ${c.y}, ${c.x+(n.x-c.x)*0.5} ${n.y}, ${n.x} ${n.y}`;
    }
    return `<svg viewBox="0 0 80 80"><path d="${d}"></path></svg>`;
}

const getNoise = (len) => Array.from({length: Math.max(len, 0)}, () => CYRILLIC[Math.floor(Math.random() * CYRILLIC.length)]).join('');
// --- СИСТЕМА ПЛАВНОЙ ОТРИСОВКИ (УНИВЕРСАЛЬНАЯ) ---
const DRAW_SPEED = 100; // Скорость отрисовки

window.renderSmoothPath = function(pathId, indices, isContinuing = false) {
    const pathElem = document.getElementById(pathId);
    if (!pathElem) return;

    // 1. Получаем данные пути через твою функцию createSVG
    const svgMarkup = createSVG(indices);
    if (!svgMarkup) {
        pathElem.setAttribute('d', '');
        return;
    }
    
    // Извлекаем только содержимое атрибута d="..."
    const dMatch = svgMarkup.match(/d="([^"]*)"/);
    const dData = dMatch ? dMatch[1] : '';

    if (indices.length <= 1) {
        pathElem.setAttribute('d', dData);
        pathElem.style.strokeDasharray = 'none';
        return;
    }

    // 2. ПОДГОТОВКА АНИМАЦИИ
    pathElem.setAttribute('d', dData);
    const totalLength = pathElem.getTotalLength();
    pathElem.style.transition = 'none';

    if (isContinuing) {
        // Логика для ручного рисования (дорисовываем сегмент)
        const prevIndices = indices.slice(0, -1);
        const prevMarkup = createSVG(prevIndices);
        const prevDMatch = prevMarkup.match(/d="([^"]*)"/);
        const prevD = prevDMatch ? prevDMatch[1] : '';
        
        pathElem.setAttribute('d', prevD);
        const prevLength = pathElem.getTotalLength();
        
        pathElem.setAttribute('d', dData);
        pathElem.style.strokeDasharray = totalLength;
        pathElem.style.strokeDashoffset = totalLength - prevLength;
        
        const duration = (totalLength - prevLength) / DRAW_SPEED;
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            pathElem.style.transition = `stroke-dashoffset ${duration}s linear`;
            pathElem.style.strokeDashoffset = '0';
        });
    } else {
        // Полная отрисовка (например, при вводе текста)
        pathElem.style.strokeDasharray = totalLength;
        pathElem.style.strokeDashoffset = totalLength;
        const duration = totalLength / DRAW_SPEED;
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            pathElem.style.transition = `stroke-dashoffset ${duration}s linear`;
            pathElem.style.strokeDashoffset = '0';
        });
    }
};

// Функция для генерации недешифрованного текста (Подменная соль)
function getWrongLabel(word) {
    // 1. Если правильная соль уже найдена (глобальный фикс), возвращаем всё как есть
    if (localStorage.getItem('s9_salt_found') === 'true') return word;

    // 2. Если слово есть в VOCABULARY (эталонная выборка), оно не шифруется, 
    // так как физически записано в памяти терминала.
    if (typeof VOCABULARY !== 'undefined' && VOCABULARY.includes(word)) {
        return word;
    }

    // 3. Всё остальное шифруем (Подменная соль 7777)
    let hash = 7777;
    for (let i = 0; i < word.length; i++) hash = ((hash << 5) + hash) + word.charCodeAt(i);
    let seed = Math.abs(hash);
    
    const cyrillic = "АБВГДЕЁЖЗИЙКЛМНОПРСТУФХЦЧШЩЪЫЬЭЮЯ";
    let res = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < word.length; i++) {
        seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff;
        res += cyrillic[seed % cyrillic.length];
    }
    return res;
}

            const qubitsData = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('s9_os_qubits_data') || '{}');
            let broken = 0, warning = 0, ent = 0;

            if (qubitsData && qubitsData[1]) {
                for (let l = 1; l <= 5; l++) {
                    for (let r = 1; r <= 5; r++) {
                        for (let c = 1; c <= 5; c++) {
                            const state = qubitsData[l]?.[r]?.[c]?.state;
                            if (state === 'broken') broken++;
                            if (state === 'warning') warning++;
                            if (state === 'entangled') ent++;
                        }
                    }
                }

            if (broken === 0 && warning === 0 && ent === 0) {

                    { q: "> ОС СТАБИЛЬНА", a: "ВСЕ КУБИТЫ В РАБОЧЕМ СОСТОЯНИИ." }
                ];

            if (broken > 0) {
                msgs.push({ q: "> СЛОМАННЫЕ", a: "ИСПОЛЬЗУЙТЕ <cmd>os --reset qubit LxRxCx</cmd>." });
            }
            if (warning > 0) {
                msgs.push({ q: "> ВАРНИНГ", a: "ИСПОЛЬЗУЙТЕ <cmd>os --warn qubit LxRxCx</cmd> ДЛЯ СТАБИЛИЗАЦИИ." });
            }
            if (ent > 0) {
                msgs.push({ q: "> ЗАПУТАННЫЕ", a: "ИСПОЛЬЗУЙТЕ <cmd>os --entangle LxRxCx LxRxCx</cmd>." });
            }
            
            return msgs;
        }

        if (page === 'telemetry.html') {

                { q: "> ДИАГНОСТИКА", a: "ТРИ ЦВЕТОВЫЕ ВОЛНЫ ПОТЕРЯЛИ ПРИВЯЗКУ К ЭТАЛОНУ. ДЛЯ СТАБИЛИЗАЦИИ НЕОБХОДИМО СОВМЕСТИТЬ ИХ ПО ПАРАМЕТРАМ." },
                { q: "> РЕШЕНИЕ", a: "ИСПОЛЬЗУЙТЕ КОНСОЛЬ УПРАВЛЕНИЯ ДЛЯ ВЫВОДА ЭТАЛОНА И НАСТРОЙКИ ВОЛН." }

    function playTypeSound() {
        if (typeof audioCtx === 'undefined' || !audioCtx || (typeof isMuted !== 'undefined' && isMuted)) return;
        try {
            const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
            const g = audioCtx.createGain();
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(12000 + Math.random() * 800, audioCtx.currentTime);
            g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.008, audioCtx.currentTime);
            g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, audioCtx.currentTime + 0.01);
            osc.connect(g).connect(masterGain);
            osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + 0.01);
        } catch(e) {}
    }

    function typeHtmlText(element, htmlText, onComplete) {
        element.innerHTML = ''; 
        const tempDiv = document.createElement('div');
        tempDiv.innerHTML = htmlText;
        const nodes = Array.from(tempDiv.childNodes);
        let nodeIndex = 0;
        let charIndex = 0;
        let currentWrapper = null;
        let currentNodeText = '';

        function typeNextChar() {
            if (nodeIndex >= nodes.length) {
                if (onComplete) onComplete();
                return;
            }

            const node = nodes[nodeIndex];
            if (!currentWrapper) {
                if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
                    currentWrapper = document.createElement('span');
                    element.appendChild(currentWrapper);
                } else if (node.nodeName.toLowerCase() === 'cmd') {
                    currentWrapper = document.createElement('span');
                    currentWrapper.className = 'cmd-text';
                    element.appendChild(currentWrapper);
                } else {
                    nodeIndex++;
                    typeNextChar();
                    return;
                }
                currentNodeText = node.textContent;
                charIndex = 0;
            }

            if (charIndex < currentNodeText.length) {
                const char = currentNodeText[charIndex];
                currentWrapper.textContent += char;
                if (char !== " ") playTypeSound();
                charIndex++;
                setTimeout(typeNextChar, 30);
            } else {
                nodeIndex++;
                currentWrapper = null;
                typeNextChar();
            }
        }
        if (nodes.length > 0) typeNextChar();
        else if (onComplete) onComplete();
    }

    window.updateQuestion = function() {
        const qField = document.getElementById('quest-field');
        const aField = document.getElementById('answer-field');
        const container = document.querySelector('.chat-system');
        if (!qField || !aField) return;

        let page = window.location.pathname.split('/').pop() || 'index.html';
        
        if (page === 'archive.html') {
            const isFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_archive_fixed') === 'true';
            if (window.lastArchiveFixedState !== isFixed) {
                window.currentChatStep = 0;
                window.lastArchiveFixedState = isFixed;
            }
        }

        if (page === 'map.html') {
            const coreOverheated = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_overheated') === 'true';
            const coreFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true';
            let status = 'WAIT';
            if (coreOverheated && !coreFixed) status = 'ERROR';
            if (coreFixed) status = 'FIXED';

            if (lastCoreStatus !== status) {
                window.currentChatStep = 0;
                lastCoreStatus = status;
            }
        }

        const data = getActiveData();

        if (window.currentChatStep < data.length) {
            aField.innerText = "";
            qField.innerText = data[window.currentChatStep].q.replace(/<\/?cmd>/g, ''); 
            qField.style.opacity = "1";
            qField.style.cursor = "pointer";
            if (container) container.classList.remove('chat-fade-out');
            isTyping = false;
        } else {
            if (page === 'map.html') {
                const osFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_os_fixed') === 'true';
                const coreOverheated = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_overheated') === 'true';
                const coreFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true';
                const timerStart = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_timer_start');

                if (osFixed && !coreOverheated && !coreFixed && timerStart) {
					const remainingMs = Math.max(0, 64000 - (Date.now() - parseInt(timerStart, 10)));
					const bells = (remainingMs / 1337.3).toFixed(4);

					qField.innerText = "> ОХЛАЖДЕНИЕ ЯДРА";
					aField.innerHTML = `<span id="cooling-timer">ОСТАЛОСЬ ВРЕМЕНИ: ${bells} БЕЛЛ</span>`;

                    
                    qField.style.cursor = "default";
                    return;
                }
            }
            // ... (код кнопки "ОБНОВИТЬ" идет ниже) ...

            qField.innerHTML = `
                <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; opacity: 0.7;">
                    <span>> ЖУРНАЛ ПРОЧИТАН</span>
                    <button onclick="resetChat()" style="
                        background: rgba(0, 242, 255, 0.05); 
                        border: 1px solid rgba(0, 242, 255, 0.4); 
                        color: var(--neon); 
                        font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace; 
                        font-size: 9px; 
                        padding: 4px 8px; 
                        cursor: pointer; 
                        text-transform: uppercase; 
                        letter-spacing: 1px;
                        transition: all 0.3s ease;
                    " onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(0, 242, 255, 0.2)'; this.style.boxShadow='0 0 10px var(--neon)'" 
                       onmouseout="this.style.background='rgba(0, 242, 255, 0.05)'; this.style.boxShadow='none'">
                        [ ОБНОВИТЬ ]
                    </button>
                </div>
            `;
            aField.innerHTML = "";
            qField.style.cursor = "default";
        }
    };

    window.resetChat = function() {
        window.currentChatStep = 0;
        window.updateQuestion();
    };

    window.handleChatClick = function() {
        const data = getActiveData();
        if (isTyping || window.currentChatStep >= data.length) return;
        
        isTyping = true;
        const aField = document.getElementById('answer-field');
        const text = data[window.currentChatStep].a;

        typeHtmlText(aField, text, () => {
            setTimeout(() => {
                window.currentChatStep++;

                let page = window.location.pathname.split('/').pop() || 'index.html';
                
                // --- УСТАНОВКА ФЛАГОВ ПРОЧТЕНИЯ ---
                if (window.currentChatStep === data.length) {
                    if (page === 'tools.html' && localStorage.getItem('s9_archive_calibrated') === 'true') localStorage.setItem('s9_read_success_tools', 'true');
                    if (page === 'archive.html' && localStorage.getItem('s9_archive_fixed') === 'true') localStorage.setItem('s9_read_success_archive', 'true');
                    if (page === 'os.html' && localStorage.getItem('s9_os_fixed') === 'true') localStorage.setItem('s9_read_success_os', 'true');
                    if (page === 'telemetry.html' && localStorage.getItem('s9_telemetry_calibrated') === 'true') localStorage.setItem('s9_read_success_telemetry', 'true');
                    if (page === 'core.html' && localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true') localStorage.setItem('s9_read_success_core', 'true');
                }
                // ----------------------------------

                if (page === 'index.html' && window.currentChatStep === data.length) {
                    if (localStorage.getItem('s9_lang_human') !== 'true') {
                        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('autoOpenConsole'));
                    }
                }

                // Запускаем таймер ДО обновления вопроса
                if (page === 'map.html' && window.currentChatStep === data.length) {
                    localStorage.setItem('s9_map_visited', 'true');
                    
                    const osFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_os_fixed') === 'true';
                    const coreOverheated = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_overheated') === 'true';
                    const coreFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true';
                    
                    if (osFixed && !coreOverheated && !coreFixed && !localStorage.getItem('s9_core_timer_start')) {
                        localStorage.setItem('s9_core_timer_start', Date.now().toString());
                    }
                }

                isTyping = false; // ВАЖНО: Сбрасываем флаг печати
                window.updateQuestion();
            }, 2000);
        });
    };

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', window.updateQuestion);
    } else {
        window.updateQuestion();
    }

    setInterval(() => {
        const page = window.location.pathname.split('/').pop() || 'index.html';
        if (page === 'map.html') {
            const osFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_os_fixed') === 'true';
            const coreOverheated = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_overheated') === 'true';
            const coreFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true';
            const timerStart = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_timer_start');
            
            if (osFixed && !coreOverheated && !coreFixed && timerStart) {
                const timerEl = document.getElementById('cooling-timer');
					if (timerEl) {
						const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now() - parseInt(timerStart, 10)) / 1000);
						const remaining = Math.max(0, 64 - elapsed);
						const remainingMs = Math.max(0, 64000 - (Date.now() - parseInt(timerStart, 10)));
						timerEl.innerText = `ОСТАЛОСЬ ВРЕМЕНИ: ${(remainingMs / 1337.3).toFixed(4)} БЕЛЛ`;
						
						if (remaining <= 0) {
                        localStorage.setItem('s9_core_overheated', 'true');
                        localStorage.removeItem('s9_core_timer_start');
                        location.reload();
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }, 1000);
// ПЛАВНЫЙ ОТСЧЁТ ОХЛАЖДЕНИЯ В БЕЛЛАХ (ОБНОВЛЕНИЕ КАЖДЫЕ 100 МС)
setInterval(() => {
    const timerEl = document.getElementById('cooling-timer');
    if (!timerEl) return;
    const timerStart = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_timer_start');
    if (!timerStart) return;
    if (localStorage.getItem('s9_core_overheated') === 'true' || localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true') return;
    const remainingMs = Math.max(0, 64000 - (Date.now() - parseInt(timerStart, 10)));
    timerEl.innerText = `ОСТАЛОСЬ ВРЕМЕНИ: ${(remainingMs / 1337.3).toFixed(4)} БЕЛЛ`;
}, 50);

})();

        "js/cheats.js",
        "js/sync.js" 
    ];
    scripts.forEach(src => {
        document.write(`<script src="${src}"></script>`);
    });
    console.log("SYSTEM: Все модули ядра загружены.");
})();

====================================== end loader.js ====================================== 

====================================== start map.js ====================================== 
FILE: D:\OSPanel\home\signal-9.local\public\js\map.js 
// js/map.js
(function() {

    const MAP_DATA = {
        nodes: [
            { id: 'hub', title: 'ГЛАВНЫЙ\nХАБ', x: 600, y: 425, url: 'index.html', type: 'always' },
            { id: 'tools', title: 'ИНЖЕНЕРНЫЙ\nТЕРМИНАЛ', x: 600, y: 200, url: 'tools.html', type: 'always' },
            { id: 'map', title: 'КАРТА', x: 600, y: 650, url: 'map.html', type: 'always' },

            // ОС (ждет починки архива)
            { id: 'os', title: 'ОПЕРАЦИОННАЯ\nСИСТЕМА', x: 350, y: 200, url: 'os.html', type: 'os' },
            
            // Архив (красный, если сломан)
            { id: 'archive', title: 'БАЗА\nДАННЫХ', x: 350, y: 650, url: 'archive.html', type: 'archive' },

            // Остальные (заглушки/будущие квесты)
            { id: 'telemetry', title: 'МОДУЛЬ\nТЕЛЕМЕТРИИ', x: 150, y: 425, url: 'telemetry.html', type: 'offline' },
            { id: 'core', title: 'ЭНЕРГОЯДРО', x: 350, y: 425, url: 'core.html', type: 'offline' },
            { id: 'edr', title: 'EDR\nСЕКТОР', x: 850, y: 425, url: 'edr.html', type: 'offline' },
            { id: 'gateway', title: 'ТРАНСПОРТНЫЙ\nШЛЮЗ', x: 1050, y: 425, url: 'gateway.html', type: 'offline' },
            { id: 'sector00', title: 'СЕКТОР\n[0x00]', x: 850, y: 200, url: '#', type: 'offline' },
            { id: 'sectorff', title: 'СЕКТОР\n[0xFF]', x: 850, y: 650, url: '#', type: 'offline' },
        ],
        // Удаляем MAP_POINTS и getMapPath полностью.
        // Вместо них используем глобальные POINTS и getPath.
        // В MAP_DATA.nodes меняем reactor на core.
        // В links используем новую сетку:
        links: [
            { s: 'hub', t: 'tools' }, { s: 'hub', t: 'map' }, 
            { s: 'hub', t: 'core' }, { s: 'hub', t: 'edr' },
            { s: 'tools', t: 'os' }, { s: 'tools', t: 'sector00' },
            { s: 'core', t: 'telemetry' }, { s: 'core', t: 'os' }, { s: 'core', t: 'archive' },
            { s: 'edr', t: 'gateway' }, { s: 'edr', t: 'sector00' }, { s: 'edr', t: 'sectorff' },
            { s: 'map', t: 'archive' }, { s: 'map', t: 'sectorff' },
        ]
    };

    function getNodeStatus(node) {
        if (node.type === 'always') return 'ONLINE';

        if (node.type === 'archive') {
            return localStorage.getItem('s9_archive_fixed') === 'true' ? 'ONLINE' : 'ERROR';
        }

        if (node.id === 'core') {
            const osFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_os_fixed') === 'true';
            if (!osFixed) return 'OFFLINE'; // Ядро не может быть онлайн, пока ОС не починена

            const isOverheated = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_overheated') === 'true';
            const isFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true';

            if (isFixed) return 'ONLINE';
            if (isOverheated) return 'ERROR';
            return 'WARNING'; // Желтый статус (период охлаждения)
        }

        if (node.type === 'os') {
            const isArchiveFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_archive_fixed') === 'true';
            if (!isArchiveFixed) return 'OFFLINE';
            return localStorage.getItem('s9_os_fixed') === 'true' ? 'ONLINE' : 'ERROR';
        }

        // Проверка флагов для остальных
        if (node.id === 'telemetry') {
            const coreFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true';
            if (!coreFixed) return 'OFFLINE'; // Нельзя войти, пока Ядро не починено
            
            const isTelemetryFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_telemetry_calibrated') === 'true';
            return isTelemetryFixed ? 'ONLINE' : 'ERROR'; // Красный, пока не синхронизировали
        }
        if (node.id === 'edr' && localStorage.getItem('s9_os_fixed') === 'true') return 'ONLINE';
        //if (node.id === 'gateway' && localStorage.getItem('s9_core_unlocked') === 'true') return 'ONLINE';
        
        return 'OFFLINE';
    }

    function buildMap() {
        const svg = document.getElementById('mapLines');
        const nodesContainer = document.getElementById('mapNodes');
        if (!svg || !nodesContainer) return;

        svg.innerHTML = '';
        nodesContainer.innerHTML = '';

        // Рисуем линии
        MAP_DATA.links.forEach(link => {
            const source = MAP_DATA.nodes.find(n => n.id === link.s);
            const target = MAP_DATA.nodes.find(n => n.id === link.t);
            if (!source || !target) return;

            const sourceStatus = getNodeStatus(source);
            const targetStatus = getNodeStatus(target);
            
            // Логика состояний линии
            let lineClass = 'link-locked'; // По умолчанию тусклая
            let isActive = false;

            // Если хотя бы один узел OFFLINE, линия должна быть тусклой (или скрытой), независимо от ERROR
            if (sourceStatus === 'OFFLINE' || targetStatus === 'OFFLINE') {
                lineClass = 'link-locked';
                isActive = false;
            } else if (sourceStatus === 'ERROR' || targetStatus === 'ERROR') {
                lineClass = 'link-error';
                isActive = true;
            } else if (sourceStatus === 'ONLINE' && targetStatus === 'ONLINE') {
                lineClass = 'link-active';
                isActive = true;
            } else if (sourceStatus === 'WARNING' || targetStatus === 'WARNING') {
                lineClass = 'link-locked'; // Или можно сделать желтую линию, если хочешь
                isActive = true;
            }

            const g = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'g');
            g.setAttribute('class', lineClass);

            const bgLine = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'line');
            bgLine.setAttribute('x1', source.x); bgLine.setAttribute('y1', source.y);
            bgLine.setAttribute('x2', target.x); bgLine.setAttribute('y2', target.y);
            bgLine.setAttribute('class', 'link-bg');
            g.appendChild(bgLine);

            if (isActive) {
                const flowLine = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'line');
                flowLine.setAttribute('x1', source.x); flowLine.setAttribute('y1', source.y);
                flowLine.setAttribute('x2', target.x); flowLine.setAttribute('y2', target.y);
                flowLine.setAttribute('class', 'link-flow');
                flowLine.style.animationDelay = `${Math.random() * -20}s`;
                g.appendChild(flowLine);
            }
            svg.appendChild(g);
        });

        // Рисуем узлы
        MAP_DATA.nodes.forEach(node => {
            const status = getNodeStatus(node);
            
            let isClickable = (status !== 'OFFLINE' && status !== 'WARNING');
            if (node.id === 'archive' && localStorage.getItem('s9_map_visited') !== 'true') {
                isClickable = false;
            }
            
            const el = document.createElement(isClickable ? 'a' : 'div');
            if (isClickable) {
                if (node.id === 'gateway') {
                    // Если шлюз разблокирован, проверяем пройден ли EDR
                    if (localStorage.getItem('s9_edr_gateway_passed') === 'true') {
                        el.href = 'gateway.html';
                    } else {
                        // Если не пройден - ставим триггер и ведем на взлом
                        el.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            localStorage.setItem('s9_edr_gateway_trigger', 'true');
                            window.location.href = 'edr.html';
                        };
                    }
                } else {
                    el.href = node.url;
                }
            }
            
            // ДОБАВЛЯЕМ КЛАСС, ЕСЛИ УЗЕЛ НЕКЛИКАБЕЛЕН
            let cssClass = 'map-node ';
            if (status === 'ONLINE') cssClass += 'unlocked';
            else if (status === 'ERROR') cssClass += 'error-node';
            else if (status === 'WARNING') cssClass += 'warning-node'; /* Добавили желтый статус */
            else cssClass += 'locked'; 
            
            if (!isClickable) cssClass += ' no-pointer'; // <--- ВОТ ЭТА СТРОКА

            el.className = cssClass;
            // ... дальше идет el.innerHTML ...
            el.setAttribute('data-id', node.id);
            el.style.left = `${node.x}px`;
            el.style.top = `${node.y}px`;

            const formattedTitle = node.title.replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
            
            const wordForGlyph = node.title.split('\n')[0].replace(/\[.*?\]/g, '').trim();
            const glyphSVG = createSVG(getPath(wordForGlyph));

                    // ... (здесь заканчивается цикл MAP_DATA.nodes.forEach) ...
            
            el.innerHTML = `
                <div class="node-glyph">${glyphSVG}</div>
                <div class="node-title">${formattedTitle}</div>
                <div class="node-status">${status}</div>
            `;
            nodesContainer.appendChild(el);
        }); // <--- КОНЕЦ ЦИКЛА MAP_DATA.nodes.forEach

        // --- Декоративный прямоугольник для Телеметрии ---
        const telemetryNode = MAP_DATA.nodes.find(n => n.id === 'telemetry');
        if (telemetryNode) {
            const decor = document.createElement('div');
            decor.className = 'decor-rect';
            
            // Вычисляем позицию: 
            // От центра Телеметрии (x) отнимаем половину её ширины (65), 
            // отнимаем отступ (5) и отнимаем половину ширины нашего прямоугольника (7).
            const decorX = telemetryNode.x - 65 - 5 - 7;
            const decorY = telemetryNode.y; // Выравниваем по вертикали

            decor.style.left = `${decorX}px`;
            decor.style.top = `${decorY}px`;
            
            nodesContainer.appendChild(decor);
        }
        // ------------------------------------------------

    } // <--- КОНЕЦ ФУНКЦИИ buildMap

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', buildMap);
	
})();

====================================== end map.js ====================================== 

====================================== start os.js ====================================== 
FILE: D:\OSPanel\home\signal-9.local\public\js\os.js 
// js/os.js
(function() {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const grid = document.getElementById('qubitGrid');
        const logBox = document.getElementById('logBox');
        const desktop = document.getElementById('os-desktop');
        const tabsContainer = document.getElementById('layerTabs');
        const gridWrapper = document.getElementById('gridWrapper');

        let activeLayer = 1;
        let qubits = {};
        let isAnimating = false;
		
        // --- ЛОГИ ---
        function addLog(text, type = '') {
            if (!logBox) return;
            const line = document.createElement('div');
            line.className = `log-line ${type}`;
            line.innerText = text;
            logBox.appendChild(line);
            if (logBox.children.length > 24) logBox.removeChild(logBox.firstChild);
            logBox.scrollTop = logBox.scrollHeight;
        }

        function updateStats() {
            const valCpu = document.getElementById('valCpu');
            const valRam = document.getElementById('valRam');
            const barCpu = document.getElementById('barCpu');
            const barRam = document.getElementById('barRam');
            if (!valCpu) return; 

            const cpu = Math.floor(Math.random() * 40) + 30;
            const ram = Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 20;

            valCpu.innerText = cpu + '%';
            valRam.innerText = ram + '%';
            barCpu.style.width = cpu + '%';
            barRam.style.width = ram + '%';

            if (cpu > 60) barCpu.classList.add('warn'); else barCpu.classList.remove('warn');
            if (ram > 60) barRam.classList.add('warn'); else barRam.classList.remove('warn');
        }

        // --- 1. ВЕРИФИКАЦИЯ ---
        const isCaptchaPassed = localStorage.getItem('s9_os_captcha') === 'true';
        const shield = document.getElementById('os-shield-overlay');

        if (isCaptchaPassed) {
            if (shield) shield.style.display = 'none';
            desktop.style.display = 'flex';
            initGame();
        } else {
            if (shield) shield.style.display = 'flex';
            desktop.style.display = 'none';
        }

        // --- 2. ЛОГИКА 5x5x5 ---
        function initGame() {
            if (!grid) return;
            
            let savedData = localStorage.getItem('s9_os_qubits_data');
            if (savedData) {
                qubits = JSON.parse(savedData);
            } else {
                generateRandomQubits();
            }

            renderTabs();
            renderGrid();
            startMonitorLoop();
        }

        function generateRandomQubits() {
            qubits = {};
            for (let l = 1; l <= 5; l++) {
                qubits[l] = {};
                for (let r = 1; r <= 5; r++) {
                    qubits[l][r] = {};
                    for (let c = 1; c <= 5; c++) qubits[l][r][c] = { state: 'active' };
                }
            }

            const pool = [];
            for (let l = 1; l <= 5; l++)
                for (let r = 1; r <= 5; r++)
                    for (let c = 1; c <= 5; c++) pool.push({ l, r, c });
                       // Правильный shuffle (Fisher-Yates)
            for (let i = pool.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
                [pool[i], pool[j]] = [pool[j], pool[i]];
            }

            // 4 сломанных
            for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                const p = pool.pop();
                qubits[p.l][p.r][p.c].state = 'broken';
            }
            
            // 2 пары запутанных
            for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
                const p1 = pool.pop(), p2 = pool.pop();
                const type = i === 0 ? 'A' : 'B';
                qubits[p1.l][p1.r][p1.c] = { state: 'entangled', pair: type, linkedTo: `L${p2.l}R${p2.r}C${p2.c}` };
                qubits[p2.l][p2.r][p2.c] = { state: 'entangled', pair: type, linkedTo: `L${p1.l}R${p1.r}C${p1.c}` };
            }
            
            // 2 желтых
            for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
                const p = pool.pop();
                qubits[p.l][p.r][p.c] = { state: 'warning' };
            }

            // 25 серых
            for (let i = 0; i < 25; i++) {
                const p = pool.pop();
                qubits[p.l][p.r][p.c].state = 'locked';
            }

            saveState();
        }

        function saveState() {
            localStorage.setItem('s9_os_qubits_data', JSON.stringify(qubits));
        }

        // --- 3. ОТРИСОВКА ---
        function renderTabs() {
            tabsContainer.innerHTML = '';
            for (let l = 1; l <= 5; l++) {
                const tab = document.createElement('div');
                tab.className = 'layer-tab';
                if (l === activeLayer) tab.classList.add('active');

                let dotClass = 'dot-clean';
                let hasBroken = false, hasWarn = false, hasEnt = false;

                for (let r = 1; r <= 5; r++) for (let c = 1; c <= 5; c++) {
                    const s = qubits[l][r][c].state;
                    if (s === 'broken') hasBroken = true;
                    else if (s === 'warning') hasWarn = true;
                    else if (s === 'entangled') hasEnt = true;
                }
                
                if (hasBroken) dotClass = 'dot-err';
                else if (hasWarn) dotClass = 'dot-warn';
                else if (hasEnt) dotClass = 'dot-ent';

                tab.innerHTML = `L${l}<div class="status-dot ${dotClass}"></div>`;
                tab.onclick = () => switchLayer(l);
                tabsContainer.appendChild(tab);
            }
        }

        function renderGrid() {
            grid.innerHTML = '';
            for (let r = 1; r <= 5; r++) {
                for (let c = 1; c <= 5; c++) {
                    const q = qubits[activeLayer][r][c];
                    const el = document.createElement('div');
                    el.className = 'qubit';

                    if (q.state === 'broken') el.classList.add('q-broken');
                    else if (q.state === 'warning') el.classList.add('q-warn');
                    else if (q.state === 'entangled') el.classList.add('q-ent');
                    else if (q.state === 'locked') {
                        el.style.opacity = '0.2'; el.style.borderColor = '#555'; el.style.boxShadow = 'none';
                    }

                    if (q.state === 'entangled') {
                        const dot = document.createElement('div');
                        dot.className = `pair-dot pair-${q.pair}`;
                        el.appendChild(dot);
                    }
                    grid.appendChild(el);
                }
            }
        }

        // --- 4. АНИМАЦИЯ ПЕРЕХОДА ---
        function switchLayer(target) {
            if (isAnimating || target === activeLayer) return;

            isAnimating = true;
            const isForward = target > activeLayer;

            gridWrapper.className = `grid-wrapper ${isForward ? 'anim-out-past' : 'anim-out-future'}`;

            setTimeout(() => {
                activeLayer = target;
                renderGrid();
                const valLayer = document.getElementById('valLayer');
                if (valLayer) valLayer.innerText = `${String(activeLayer).padStart(2,'0')} / 05`;
                renderTabs();

                gridWrapper.className = `grid-wrapper ${isForward ? 'anim-in-future' : 'anim-in-past'}`;

                setTimeout(() => {
                    gridWrapper.className = 'grid-wrapper';
                    isAnimating = false;
                }, 600);
            }, 600);
        }

        // --- 5. ПРОВЕРКИ И ПОБЕДА ---
        function checkAllFixed() {
            let totalProblems = 0;
            for (let l = 1; l <= 5; l++)
                for (let r = 1; r <= 5; r++)
                    for (let c = 1; c <= 5; c++) {
                        const s = qubits[l][r][c].state;
                        if (s === 'broken' || s === 'warning' || s === 'entangled') totalProblems++;
                    }

            if (totalProblems === 0) {
                // Отключаем повторные вызовы
                if (window.osWinTriggered) return;
                window.osWinTriggered = true;
                
                addLog(`> FATAL: CRITICAL SYNC FAILURE. EDR REQUIRED.`, "err");
                if (window.printToConsole) {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">[ОС] КРИТИЧЕСКИЙ СБОЙ СИНХРОНИЗАЦИИ ЯДРА. ТРЕБУЕТСЯ ПРОВЕРКА EDR.</span>`);
                }
                
                // Пауза 2 секунды перед EDR
                setTimeout(() => { 
                    localStorage.setItem('s9_edr_os_trigger', 'true');
                    window.location.href = 'edr.html'; 
                }, 2000);
            }
        }

        // --- 6. КОМАНДЫ ИЗ КОНСОЛИ ---
        window.processOsCommand = function(args) {
            // args - массив строк, например ['--reset', 'qubit', 'l1r1c1']
            
            const action = args[0];
            const isPair = args.length === 4;

            if (action === '--reset' && args[1] === 'qubit') {
                if (args.length !== 3) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЙ ФОРМАТ. ПРИМЕР: os --reset qubit L1R1C1</span>`); return; }
                
                const m = args[2].match(/^l(\d)r(\d)c(\d)$/i);
                if (!m) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЙ ФОРМАТ.</span>`); return; }

                const l = +m[1], r = +m[2], c = +m[3];
                const q = qubits[l]?.[r]?.[c];
                if (!q) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЕ КООРДИНАТЫ.</span>`); return; }

                if (q.state === 'entangled') {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: КВАНТОВАЯ СВЯЗЬ. ВВЕДИТЕ: os --entangle LxRxCx LxRxCx</span>`); return;
                }
                if (q.state === 'warning') {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: ДЕКОГЕРЕНЦИЯ. ВВЕДИТЕ: os --warn qubit LxRxCx</span>`); return;
                }
                if (q.state === 'active' || q.state === 'locked') {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">КУБИТ УЖЕ В НОРМЕ.</span>`); return;
                }

                q.state = 'active';
                saveState();
                if (activeLayer === l) renderGrid();
                renderTabs();
                addLog(`> SYS: QUBIT L${l}R${r}C${c} RESTORED.`, "ok");
                window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[УСПЕХ] КУБИТ L${l}R${r}C${c} ВОССТАНОВЛЕН.</span>`);
                checkAllFixed();
            } 
            
            else if (action === '--entangle') {
                if (args.length !== 3) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЙ ФОРМАТ. ПРИМЕР: os --entangle L1R1C1 L2R2C2</span>`); return; }
                
                const m1 = args[1].match(/^l(\d)r(\d)c(\d)$/i);
                const m2 = args[2].match(/^l(\d)r(\d)c(\d)$/i);
                if (!m1 || !m2) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЙ ФОРМАТ.</span>`); return; }

                const [_, l1, r1, c1] = m1;
                const [__, l2, r2, c2] = m2;
                const q1 = qubits[+l1]?.[+r1]?.[+c1];
                const q2 = qubits[+l2]?.[+r2]?.[+c2];

                if (!q1 || !q2 || q1.state !== 'entangled' || q2.state !== 'entangled' || q1.pair !== q2.pair) {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: ЭТИ КУБИТЫ НЕ СВЯЗАНЫ.</span>`); return;
                }

                q1.state = 'active'; q2.state = 'active';
                saveState();
                if (activeLayer === +l1 || activeLayer === +l2) renderGrid();
                renderTabs();
                addLog(`> SYS: QUANTUM PAIR [${q1.pair}] COLLAPSED.`, "ok");
                window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[УСПЕХ] СВЯЗЬ РАЗОРВАНА. ОБА КУБИТА В НОРМЕ.</span>`);
                checkAllFixed();
            } 
            
            else if (action === '--warn' && args[1] === 'qubit') {
                if (args.length !== 3) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЙ ФОРМАТ. ПРИМЕР: os --warn qubit L1R1C1</span>`); return; }
                
                const m = args[2].match(/^l(\d)r(\d)c(\d)$/i);
                if (!m) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЙ ФОРМАТ.</span>`); return; }

                const l = +m[1], r = +m[2], c = +m[3];
                const q = qubits[l]?.[r]?.[c];
                if (!q) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЕ КООРДИНАТЫ.</span>`); return; }

                if (q.state !== 'warning') {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: КУБИТ НЕ В СОСТОЯНИИ ПРЕДУПРЕЖДЕНИЯ.</span>`); return;
                }

                q.state = 'active';
                saveState();

                if (activeLayer === l) renderGrid();
                renderTabs();
                addLog(`> SYS: QUBIT L${l}R${r}C${c} STABILIZED.`, "ok");
                window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[УСПЕХ] КУБИТ L${l}R${r}C${c} СТАБИЛИЗИРОВАН.</span>`);
                checkAllFixed();
            }
            
            else if (action === '--move') {
                if (args.length !== 3) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЙ ФОРМАТ. ПРИМЕР: os --move L1R1C1 L5R5C5</span>`); return; }
                
                const m1 = args[1].match(/^l(\d)r(\d)c(\d)$/i);
                const m2 = args[2].match(/^l(\d)r(\d)c(\d)$/i);
                if (!m1 || !m2) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЙ ФОРМАТ.</span>`); return; }

                const [_, l1, r1, c1] = m1;
                const [__, l2, r2, c2] = m2;
                const q1 = qubits[+l1]?.[+r1]?.[+c1];
                const q2 = qubits[+l2]?.[+r2]?.[+c2];

                if (!q1 || !q2) { window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕВЕРНЫЕ КООРДИНАТЫ.</span>`); return; }

                // Разрешено двигать только active и locked
                const validStates = ['active', 'locked'];
                if (!validStates.includes(q1.state) || !validStates.includes(q2.state)) {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕЛЬЗЯ ПЕРЕМЕЩАТЬ ПОВРЕЖДЕННЫЙ КУБИТ.</span>`); return;
                }

                // Свап состояний
                const tmp = { ...q1 };
                Object.assign(q1, q2);
                Object.assign(q2, tmp);
                
                saveState();
                if (activeLayer === +l1 || activeLayer === +l2) renderGrid();
                renderTabs();
                window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[УСПЕХ] КУБИТЫ ПЕРЕМЕЩЕНЫ.</span>`);
            }
            else {
                window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">ОШИБКА: НЕИЗВЕСТНАЯ КОМАНДА.</span>`);
            }
        };

        // --- 7. МОНИТОРИНГ ---
        function startMonitorLoop() {
            for(let i=0; i<20; i++) addLog("> SYS_MONITOR: BACKGROUND PROCESS OK");
            
            if (!window.osMonitorInterval) {
                window.osMonitorInterval = setInterval(updateStats, 1500);
                updateStats(); 
                
                const logMessages = [
                    { text: "> CALIBRATING SECTOR 07...", type: "" },
                    { text: "> CHECKING MEMORY BLOCKS...", type: "" },
                    { text: "> SCANNING FOR ANOMALIES...", type: "" },
                    { text: "> DATA STREAM RECEIVED", type: "ok" },
                    { text: "> KERNEL INTEGRITY OK", type: "ok" }
                ];

                window.osLogInterval = setInterval(() => {
                    if (Math.random() > 0.5) {
                        const msg = logMessages[Math.floor(Math.random() * logMessages.length)];
                        addLog(msg.text, msg.type);
                    }
                }, 2000);
            }
        }
    });
})();
====================================== end os.js ======================================

// js/reactor.js
(function() {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const core = document.getElementById('reactorCore');
        const logBox = document.getElementById('logBox');
        const valTemp = document.getElementById('valTemp');
        const barTemp = document.getElementById('barTemp');
		const valStab = document.getElementById('valStab');
		const barStab = document.getElementById('barStab');
		const valOut = document.getElementById('valOut');
		const barOut = document.getElementById('barOut');

        // Генерация частиц для визуала
        const particlesContainer = document.getElementById('particles');
        if (particlesContainer) {
            for(let i=0; i<25; i++) {
                const p = document.createElement('div');
                p.className = 'particle';
                p.style.animationDelay = `${Math.random() * 2}s`;
                p.style.animationDuration = `${1 + Math.random() * 2}s`;
                particlesContainer.appendChild(p);
            }
        }

        function addLog(text, type = '') {
            if (!logBox) return;
            const line = document.createElement('div');
            line.className = `log-line ${type}`;
            line.innerText = text;
            logBox.appendChild(line);
            if (logBox.children.length > 17) logBox.removeChild(logBox.firstChild);
            logBox.scrollTop = logBox.scrollHeight;
        }

        // Проверяем, починено ли ядро
        const isCoreFixed = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true';

		// --- ДИНАМИЧЕСКИЕ ПОКАЗАТЕЛИ ЯДРА ---
		const coreOverheated = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_overheated') === 'true';
		const coreTimerStart = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_timer_start');
		const coreState = isCoreFixed ? 'fixed' : (coreOverheated ? 'critical' : (coreTimerStart ? 'cooling' : 'critical'));

		const RANGES = {
			fixed:    { temp: [44, 46], out: [97, 99], stab: [99, 100] },
			cooling:  { temp: [82, 88], out: [70, 75], stab: [58, 63] },
			critical: { temp: [96, 99], out: [62, 68], stab: [3, 8] }
		};
		const range = RANGES[coreState];
		const rnd = (min, max) => Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min;

		// Визуал ядра по состоянию
		if (coreState === 'fixed') {
			core.classList.remove('critical');
			core.style.background = "radial-gradient(circle, #fff 0%, #00ff44 30%, #0066ff 70%, transparent 100%)";
			core.style.boxShadow = "0 0 50px #00ff44, 0 0 100px #0066ff, inset 0 0 30px #fff";
		} else if (coreState === 'cooling') {
			core.classList.remove('critical');
			core.style.background = "radial-gradient(circle, #fff 0%, #ffaa00 30%, #ff7700 70%, transparent 100%)";
			core.style.boxShadow = "0 0 50px #ffaa00, 0 0 100px #ff7700, inset 0 0 30px #fff";
		} else {
			core.classList.add('critical');
			core.style.background = "radial-gradient(circle, #fff 0%, #ffaa00 30%, #ff3333 70%, transparent 100%)";
			core.style.boxShadow = "0 0 50px #ffaa00, 0 0 100px #ff3333, inset 0 0 30px #fff";
		}

		// Первичный рапорт монитора
		logBox.innerHTML = '';
		if (coreState === 'fixed') {
			addLog('> CORE MONITOR: ONLINE', 'ok');
			addLog('> OUTPUT: 98% — NOMINAL', 'ok');
			addLog('> STABILITY: 100% — LOCKED', 'ok');
			addLog('> MAGNETS: ALIGNED', 'ok');
			addLog('> TELEMETRY MODULE: POWERED', 'ok');
			addLog('> SYSTEM STABLE', 'ok');
		} else if (coreState === 'cooling') {
			addLog('> CORE MONITOR: ONLINE');
			addLog('> COOLING CYCLE ACTIVE', 'warn');
			const coolingLine = document.createElement('div');
			coolingLine.className = 'log-line';
			logBox.appendChild(coolingLine);
			const updateCooling = () => {
				const start = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_timer_start');
				if (!start || localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true') return;
				const remainingMs = Math.max(0, 64000 - (Date.now() - parseInt(start, 10)));
				coolingLine.innerText = `> COOLING: ${(remainingMs / 1337.3).toFixed(4)} БЕЛЛ`;
			};
			updateCooling();
			setInterval(updateCooling, 50);
		} else {
			addLog('> CORE MONITOR: ONLINE');
			addLog('> TEMP: 98% — CRITICAL', 'err');
			addLog('> STABILITY: 5% — FAILING', 'err');
			addLog('> MAGNETS: DEGRADED', 'warn');
			addLog('> MANUAL STABILIZATION REQUIRED', 'warn');
		}

		// Живые показатели — новая случайная величина каждую секунду
		function updateMeters() {
			const cls = coreState === 'fixed' ? '' : (coreState === 'cooling' ? 'warn' : 'err');
			const color = coreState === 'fixed' ? 'var(--ok)' : (coreState === 'cooling' ? 'var(--warn)' : 'var(--err)');

			if (valTemp) {
				valTemp.innerText = rnd(...range.temp) + '%';
				barTemp.style.width = valTemp.innerText;
				barTemp.className = cls ? `progress-fill ${cls}` : 'progress-fill';
			}
			if (valOut) {
				valOut.innerText = rnd(...range.out) + '%';
				barOut.style.width = valOut.innerText;
				barOut.className = cls ? `progress-fill ${cls}` : 'progress-fill';
			}
			if (valStab) {
				valStab.innerText = rnd(...range.stab) + '%';
				barStab.style.width = valStab.innerText;
				barStab.className = cls ? `progress-fill ${cls}` : 'progress-fill';
				valStab.style.color = color;
			}
		}
		updateMeters();
		setInterval(updateMeters, 1000);

		// Живой журнал монитора — свежая строка телеметрии каждые 3 секунды
		const LOG_FEED = {
			fixed: [
				() => `> TEMP: ${rnd(44, 46)}% — NOMINAL`,
				() => `> OUTPUT: ${rnd(97, 99)}% — STABLE`,
				() => `> MAGNET FIELD: ALIGNED`,
				() => `> TELEMETRY LINK: ACTIVE`
			],
			cooling: [
				() => `> TEMP: ${rnd(82, 88)}% — DROPPING`,
				() => `> OUTPUT: ${rnd(70, 75)}% — RESTRICTED`,
				() => `> COOLANT FLOW: ACTIVE`
			],
			critical: [
				() => `> TEMP: ${rnd(96, 99)}% — RISING`,
				() => `> STABILITY: ${rnd(3, 8)}% — FAILING`,
				() => `> MAGNETS: DEGRADED`,
				() => `> REACTOR OUTPUT: ${rnd(62, 68)}%`
			]
		};

		setInterval(() => {
			if (!logBox) return;
			const templates = LOG_FEED[coreState];
			addLog(templates[Math.floor(Math.random() * templates.length)](), coreState === 'fixed' ? 'ok' : coreState);
			// Строка отсчёта охлаждения всегда остаётся последней
			const coolingEl = Array.from(logBox.children).find(el => el.innerText.startsWith('> COOLING:'));
			if (coolingEl) logBox.appendChild(coolingEl);
		}, 3000);


        let targetX, targetY, targetZ;
        let sensors, expected;

		const isOverheated = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_overheated') === 'true';
		const timerStart = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_timer_start');

		if (isCoreFixed) {
			// ...зелёный рапорт выше...
		} else if (isOverheated) {
			logBox.innerHTML = '';
			addLog('> CORE MONITOR: ONLINE');
			addLog('> TEMP: 98% — CRITICAL', 'err');
			addLog('> STABILITY: 5% — FAILING', 'err');
			addLog('> MAGNETS: DEGRADED', 'warn');
			addLog('> MANUAL STABILIZATION REQUIRED', 'warn');
		} else if (timerStart) {
			logBox.innerHTML = '';
			addLog('> CORE MONITOR: ONLINE');
			addLog('> COOLING CYCLE ACTIVE', 'warn');
			const coolingLine = document.createElement('div');
			coolingLine.className = 'log-line';
			logBox.appendChild(coolingLine);
			const updateCooling = () => {
				const start = localStorage.getItem('s9_core_timer_start');
				if (!start || localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true') return;
				const remainingMs = Math.max(0, 64000 - (Date.now() - parseInt(start, 10)));
				coolingLine.innerText = `> COOLING: ${(remainingMs / 1337.3).toFixed(4)} БЕЛЛ`;
			};
			updateCooling();
			setInterval(updateCooling, 50);
		}


        if (!isCoreFixed) {
            targetX = Math.floor(Math.random() * 11) - 5;
            targetY = Math.floor(Math.random() * 11) - 5;
            targetZ = Math.floor(Math.random() * 11) - 5;
            
            if (targetX === 0) targetX = 2;
            if (targetY === 0) targetY = -3;
            if (targetZ === 0) targetZ = 1;

            sensors = {
                'rt': 10 - targetX,
                'lt': 10 + targetX,
                'up': 10 - targetY,
                'dn': 10 + targetY,
                'fw': 10 - targetZ,
                'bk': 10 + targetZ
            };

            expected = {
                'rt': 10 - sensors['rt'],
                'lt': 10 - sensors['lt'],
                'up': 10 - sensors['up'],
                'dn': 10 - sensors['dn'],
                'fw': 10 - sensors['fw'],
                'bk': 10 - sensors['bk']
            };

            window.coreEquations = {
                xy: targetX + targetY,
                yz: targetY + targetZ,
                xz: targetX + targetZ
            };

            window.coreTargets = sensors;
        }

        // 3. ФУНКЦИЯ ПРОВЕРКИ ВВЕДЕННОЙ СТРОКИ
        window.checkCorePosition = function(cmdStr) {
            if (localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true') {
                if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[OK] ЯДРО СТАБИЛЬНО. КОМАНДА НЕ ТРЕБУЕТСЯ.</span>`);
                return;
            }
				if (localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true') {
					if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[OK] ЯДРО СТАБИЛЬНО. КОМАНДА НЕ ТРЕБУЕТСЯ.</span>`);
					return;
				}
            const rtMatch = cmdStr.match(/rt\s*([+-]?\d+)/);
            const ltMatch = cmdStr.match(/lt\s*([+-]?\d+)/);
            const upMatch = cmdStr.match(/up\s*([+-]?\d+)/);
            const dnMatch = cmdStr.match(/dn\s*([+-]?\d+)/);
            const fwMatch = cmdStr.match(/fw\s*([+-]?\d+)/);
            const bkMatch = cmdStr.match(/bk\s*([+-]?\d+)/);
            if (!rtMatch || !ltMatch || !upMatch || !dnMatch || !fwMatch || !bkMatch) {
                if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">[ОШИБКА] НЕВЕРНЫЙ ФОРМАТ. ВВЕДИТЕ ВСЕ 6 ЗНАЧЕНИЙ ЧЕРЕЗ ЗАПЯТУЮ.</span>`);
                return;
            }

            const valRT = parseInt(rtMatch[1], 10);
            const valLT = parseInt(ltMatch[1], 10);
            const valUP = parseInt(upMatch[1], 10);
            const valDN = parseInt(dnMatch[1], 10);
            const valFW = parseInt(fwMatch[1], 10);
            const valBK = parseInt(bkMatch[1], 10);

            let isCorrect = true;
            let errors = [];

            if (valRT !== expected['rt']) { isCorrect = false; errors.push('RT'); }
            if (valLT !== expected['lt']) { isCorrect = false; errors.push('LT'); }
            if (valUP !== expected['up']) { isCorrect = false; errors.push('UP'); }
            if (valDN !== expected['dn']) { isCorrect = false; errors.push('DN'); }
            if (valFW !== expected['fw']) { isCorrect = false; errors.push('FW'); }
            if (valBK !== expected['bk']) { isCorrect = false; errors.push('BK'); }

            if (isCorrect) {
                if (window.printToConsole) {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[УСПЕХ] ВСЕ 6 МАГНИТОВ КАЛИБРОВАНЫ.</span>`);
                    window.printToConsole(`ВВЕДИТЕ: <span class="cmd-text">core --position sync</span> ДЛЯ ЗАПУСКА СТАБИЛИЗАЦИИ.`);
                }
                window.corePositionReady = true;
                addLog("> ALL MAGNETS CALIBRATED.", "ok");
            } else {
                if (window.printToConsole) {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">[ОШИБКА] НЕВЕРНАЯ КАЛИБРОВКА МАГНИТОВ: ${errors.join(', ')}. ПОПРОБУЙТЕ СНОВА.</span>`);
                }
                addLog("> CALIBRATION FAILED.", "err");
            }
        };

        // 4. ФУНКЦИЯ СИНХРОНИЗАЦИИ
			window.syncCore = function() {
				if (localStorage.getItem('s9_core_fixed') === 'true') {
					if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[OK] ЯДРО СТАБИЛЬНО. КОРРЕКТИРОВКА НЕ ТРЕБУЕТСЯ.</span>`);
					return;
				}
				if (!window.corePositionReady) {
                if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="err">[ОШИБКА] НЕВОЗМОЖНО СИНХРОНИЗИРОВАТЬ. СНАЧАЛА КАЛИБРУЙТЕ ВСЕ МАГНИТЫ.</span>`);
                return;
            }

            if (window.printToConsole) window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[СИСТЕМА] СИНХРОНИЗАЦИЯ ЗАПУЩЕНА...</span>`);
            
            setTimeout(() => {
                core.classList.remove('critical');
                core.style.background = "radial-gradient(circle, #fff 0%, #00ff44 30%, #0066ff 70%, transparent 100%)";
                core.style.boxShadow = "0 0 50px #00ff44, 0 0 100px #0066ff, inset 0 0 30px #fff";
                
                if (valTemp) { valTemp.innerText = "45%"; barTemp.style.width = "45%"; barTemp.className = "progress-fill"; }
                if (valStab) { valStab.innerText = "100%"; barStab.style.width = "100%"; barStab.className = "progress-fill"; valStab.style.color = "var(--ok)"; }

                localStorage.setItem('s9_core_fixed', 'true');
                localStorage.removeItem('s9_core_overheated');
                
                if (window.printToConsole) {
                    window.printToConsole(`<span class="ok">[УСПЕХ] ЭНЕРГОЯДРО СТАБИЛИЗИРОВАНО. ТЕЛЕМЕТРИЯ РАЗБЛОКИРОВАНА.</span>`);
                    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('unlockConsole'));
                }
                addLog("> CORE STABILIZED. SYSTEM ONLINE.", "ok");
                
                // Перезагрузка через 2 секунды после сообщения
               setTimeout(() => { location.reload(); }, 2000);
            }, 2000);
        };
    });
})();
